Yeon June Kang

강 연 준

 

Professor,

Department of Mechanical Engineering, Seoul National University

Major : 음향공학, 소음공학/진동공학

Office : 301-1519

Education

–  1984 ~ 1988

Seoul National University, B. S.

–  1988 ~ 1990

Seoul National University, M. S.

Purdue University, Ph. D

Honors

Distinguished Professor (Cooperation Awards), Seoul National University

International Academic Awards, The Korean Society for Noise & Vibration Engineering

Outstanding Achievements Honor, Seoul National University

Outstanding Research Award in Engineering, Seoul National University

–  2010

Excellence Award for Research and Business Development, Hyundai-Kia Motors

–  2010

Outstanding Service Award, KSME and Springer

–  2008

ICSV 15 Award(Outstanding Contribution in the Field of Sound and Vibration), IIAV

–  1993

Winner of Institute of Noise Control Engineers Student Paper Competition, NOISE-CON

–  1991 ~ 1994

Korean Government Overseas Scholarship

Experiences

– Chairman, KSNVE (The Korean Society for Noise Vibration Engineering)

– Vice-President for Asia-Pacific, I-INCE (International Institute of Noise Control Engineering)

– Director of International Collaboration, KSNVE (The Korean Society for Noise Vibration Engineering)

– Co-Technical Chair for Internoise 2015, KSNVE (The Korean Society for Noise Vibration Engineering)

– Committee Member, KOITA (Korea Industrial Technology Association)

– Advisory Committee Member, KAI (Korea Aerospace Industries)

– Chair of Department of Mechanical Engineering , KATS (Korean Agency for Technology and Standards)

– Committee Member, KATECH (Korea Automotive Technology Institute)

– Instructor, AIS (Advanced Industrial Safety Strategy Program)

– Nonexecutive director, OSSTEM IMPLANT

– Post-Doctoral Associate, Ray W. Herrick Laboratories, Purdue University

Journal / Conference Organization Activities

– JMST (Journal of Mechanical Science and Technology)

– ASA (Acoustical Society of America)

– ASK (Acoustical Society of Korea)

– IIAV (The International Institute of Acoustics and Vibration)

– INCE (Institute of Noise Control Engineering)

– KSME (The Korean Society of Mechanical Engineers)

– KSNVE (Korean Society of Noise and Vibration Engineers)

Research Interest

Acoustical Materials, Artificial Intelligence, Hybrid CAE/CAT, Road Noise, General NVH, Sound Quality

대회명 : 2025년도 추계 소음진동 학술대회
기간, 장소 : 2025/Nov, 경북 경주
참가자 : 홍은표, 남정민(서울대학교), 박상영(현대자동차), 강연준(서울대학교)
전류 기반 내부 가진력 모델을 통한 전동기 NVH 특성 분석
대회명 : 2025년도 추계 소음진동 학술대회
기간, 장소 : 2025/Nov, 경북 경주
참가자 : 우정우, 박우엽(서울대학교), 김병준(현대모비스), 강연준(서울대학교)
미세구조 분포 기반 딥러닝을 활용한 섬유형 흡음재의 음향 성능 예측
대회명 : 2025년도 한국음향학회 추계 공동학술대회
기간, 장소 : 2025/Nov, 전남 여수
